LIBNAME PLD '/riscosbvf/pld';

DATA listado_bolsa_sales;
SET listado_bolsa_sales;
    Name = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,Name);
    Name = upcase(Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,Name);
    Name = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,Name);
    Name = strip(Name);
;RUN;




 



DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
SET PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_RAIZ_ARRUMADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.CNPJ,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) AS NUM_RAIZ

      FROM WORK.'CNPJ POR NUM RAIZ'n t1
      WHERE t1.matriz_filial_enr = 'Matriz';
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_RAIZ_ARRUMADA_2 AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.CNPJ,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) AS NUM_RAIZ

      FROM WORK.'CNPJ POR NUM RAIZ'n t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_RAIZ_ARRUMADA T2 ON (T1.cnpj = T2.cnpj)
	  WHERE T2.cnpj IS MISSING;
QUIT;

DATA BS_RAIZ_ARRUMADA;
SET BS_RAIZ_ARRUMADA BS_RAIZ_ARRUMADA_2;RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BS_RAIZ_ARRUMADA NODUPKEY; BY cnpj;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_RAIZ AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
      T1.DOCCONSULTADO AS DOC_NUM,
	  T1.NOME,
      PUT(T1.DOCCONSULTADO,Z9.) AS NUM_RAIZ,
	  'PRINCIPAL' AS TP_QUADRO

   FROM PLD.BS_QSA_FIM T1

UNION ALL

   SELECT DISTINCT
      T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO AS DOC_NUM,
	  T1.NOME_QUADRO AS NOME,
      PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z9.) AS NUM_RAIZ,
	  t1.TP_QUADRO

   FROM PLD.BS_QSA_FIM T1
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_PESQUISA_RAIZ AS
   SELECT DISTINCT t1.NUM_RAIZ

      FROM WORK.PESQUISA_RAIZ t1
      WHERE t1.NUM_RAIZ NOT CONTAINS 'E' AND t1.TP_QUADRO IN
           (
           'PRINCIPAL',
           'SOCIO PJ'
           );
QUIT;




 

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_LISTADAS AS
	SELECT DISTINCT
		TRIM(LEFT(UPPER(COMPRESS(T2.'RAZÃO SOCIAL'n, , 'kasw')))) AS EMPRESA,
		UPPER(COMPRESS(T2.CNPJ,".+-*/,;?:°<>^~}]´`{[ª!@#$%&()_§|\")) AS CNPJ

FROM 'Empresas Listadas'n T2
	WHERE T2.CNPJ NOT = 'NÃO LOCALIZADO';
QUIT;

DATA LISTADO_BOLSA (WHERE=(CNPJ NOT = 'NÃO LOCALIZADO'));
SET BS_LISTADAS;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_LISTADA_BOLSA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  INPUT(T1.CNPJ,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM,
		  INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.CNPJ,1,8),BEST14.) as NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL

      FROM LISTADO_BOLSA t1

;quit;

PROC SORT DATA=BS_LISTADA_BOLSA NODUPKEY;BY _ALL_;RUN;



PROC SORT DATA=cad_fi_hist_exclusivo OUT=cad_fi_hist_exclusivo_nd; BY CNPJ_FUNDO DESCENDING DT_INI_ST_EXCLUSIVO;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=cad_fi_hist_exclusivo_nd NODUPKEY; BY CNPJ_FUNDO;RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=registro_fundo OUT=registro_fundo_ND; BY CNPJ_FUNDO DESCENDING Data_Registro;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=registro_fundo_ND NODUPKEY; BY CNPJ_FUNDO;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_ATIVO AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.CNPJ_FUNDO FORMAT BEST14. AS docConsultado,
          (CASE WHEN t2.Exclusivo = 'S' THEN 'S'
                WHEN t2.Exclusivo = 'N' THEN 'N'
                WHEN t3.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO = 'S' THEN 'S'
                WHEN t3.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO = 'N' THEN 'N' ELSE 'N' END) AS FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC,
          (CASE WHEN t1.Situacao IN ('Cancelado') THEN 'N_ATIVO' ELSE 'ATIVO' end) AS SIT_CALC

      FROM registro_fundo_ND t1
	  LEFT JOIN registro_classe T2 ON (T1.ID_Registro_Fundo = T2.ID_Registro_Fundo)
	  LEFT JOIN cad_fi_hist_exclusivo_nd T3 ON (INPUT(compress(T3.CNPJ_FUNDO,, 'kn'),BEST14.) = T1.CNPJ_FUNDO)
	  WHERE T2.Situacao NOT IN ('Cancelado')

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE cad_fi_hist_exclusivo_nd AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          INPUT(compress(CNPJ_FUNDO,, 'kn'),BEST14.)  FORMAT BEST14. AS docConsultado,
          (CASE WHEN t1.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO = 'S' THEN 'S' ELSE 'N' END) AS FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC,
          (CASE WHEN DT_FIM_ST_EXCLUSIVO IS MISSING THEN 'ATIVO' ELSE 'N_ATIVO' END) AS SIT_CALC,
          T2.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC AS FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC_2

      FROM cad_fi_hist_exclusivo_nd t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_ATIVO T2 ON (INPUT(compress(T1.CNPJ_FUNDO,, 'kn'),BEST14.) = T2.docConsultado)
	  WHERE T2.docConsultado IS MISSING AND T2.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S/INFO'

;QUIT;

DATA BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM (WHERE=(docConsultado > 0));
SET cad_fi_hist_exclusivo_nd BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_ATIVO;

RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_360_COUNT_F AS
	SELECT *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM
GROUP BY docConsultado
HAVING Count(*) > 1
;
QUIT;



PROC SORT DATA=PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP OUT=PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP_ND; BY NumCpfCnpj DESCENDING tsinclusao;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP_ND NODUPKEY; BY NumCpfCnpj;RUN;


data _null_;
   call symput('ref', put(intnx('month', today(), -1), yymmn6.));
run;


%put &ref.;

data bs_data;
REF_INI=intnx("month",TODAY(),-12);
format REF_INI  DATE9.;
put REF_INI;
run;

proc sql ;
   select distinct cat("'",put(REF_INI,DATE9.),"'","d") as REF_INI

      into :REF_INI
   from bs_data;

%LET ADM_PUBLICA = (1015,1023,1031,1040,1058,1066,1074,1082,1104,1112,1120,1139,1147,1155,1163,1171,1180,1198,

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Parametros */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 001 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Arruma Bases Onboarding Manual */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 002 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 155 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Arruma Bases Onboarding Manual */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-XZnrYJFeZuvwoyxA/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BASE_INI AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.docConsultado FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM,
		  T1.DATA_ENTRADA FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM BS_FINAL_ONBOARDING_DEMAIS  t1
	  WHERE  T1.docConsultado > 0

UNION ALL

  SELECT DISTINCT
         INPUT(compress(t1.cpf_cnpj, '.,/-', 'kd'),BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM,
         DATEPART(t1.data_de_inicio_da_etapa) FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM WORK.BS_FINAL_ONBOARDING_ATACADO T1
	  WHERE  INPUT(compress(t1.cpf_cnpj, '.,/-', 'kd'),BEST14.) > 0

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=BASE_INI;BY DOC_NUM DESCENDING DTH_CONSULTA;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=BASE_INI NODUPKEY;BY DOC_NUM;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  T2.DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM PLD.ONBOARDING_QSA  t1
      INNER JOIN BASE_INI T2 ON (T1.docConsultado = T2.DOC_NUM);

QUIT;





DATA BASE_SEM (KEEP=DOCCONSULTADO NOME);
SET ONBOARDING_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BASE_SEM NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

DATA BASE_TPPESSOA;
SET BASE_SEM;
        tipo = 'J';
		length cpf_val $11;
		cpf_val = put(DOCCONSULTADO, z11.);
		SOMA1 = (SUBSTR(cpf_val, 1,1)*10 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 2,1)*9 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 3,1)*8 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 4,1)*7 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 5,1)*6 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 6,1)*5 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 7,1)*4 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,8,1)*3 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,9,1)*2)*10;
		 	if mod(SOMA1,11)=10 then
				RESTO1 = 0;
			else
				RESTO1 = mod(SOMA1,11);

		SOMA2 = (SUBSTR(cpf_val, 1,1)*11 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 2,1)*10 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 3,1)*9 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 4,1)*8 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 5,1)*7 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 6,1)*6 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 7,1)*5 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,8,1)*4 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,9,1)*3
						+ SUBSTR(cpf_val,10,1)*2)*10;

            if mod(SOMA2,11)=10 then
				RESTO2 = 0;
			else
				RESTO2 = mod(SOMA2,11);

		dig1=(SUBSTR(cpf_val, 10,1));
        dig2=(SUBSTR(cpf_val, 11,1));
		    IF(dig1= RESTO1) AND (dig2=RESTO2)	THEN tipo = 'F';

	DROP SOMA1 SOMA2 dig1 dig2;
RUN;





DATA ONBOARDING_QSA;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA;
	NOME = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME);
	NOME= prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME);
	NOME = upcase(NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = strip(NOME);
;RUN;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA;
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO= prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = upcase(NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = strip(NOME_QUADRO);
;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
          (CASE WHEN T1.NUCONSULTADO IN ('24892393000153','19450040000244','42025234000133','45960316000163',

'20495149000104',
'78824224000105',
'41058980000160',
'73758336000191',
'35478685000160',
'17719905000119',
'72030927000185',

'39974285000105',
'83946806000102',
'46255562000187',
'39329709000170',
'31011475000107',
'45816152000103') AND TP_QUADRO IN ('SOCIO_PJ','SOCIO_PJ_ABERTO','SOCIO_PJ_FECHAD','SOCIO PF','SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'

WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (7143155000126,13342887000148,12962889000177,13102519000122,
		                   12647138000166,12652321000150,12714357000110,22575279000174,13790199000140,10314753000125,
                           19468242000132,46054404000169,43513237000189,21273336,5676632000193,42385707000103,
                           62044151000107,13288439808,3953509000147) THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (1225440300,17949903724) THEN 'SOCIO PF'
                WHEN T1.NOME_QUADRO = 'ERVIN TU' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO = '' THEN
				(CASE WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (7143155000126,13342887000148,12962889000177,13102519000122,
		                   12647138000166,12652321000150,12714357000110,22575279000174,13790199000140,10314753000125,
                           19468242000132,46054404000169,43513237000189,21273336,42385707000103,13288439808,
                           62044151000107,3953509000147) THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                           ELSE 'SOCIO PF' END)
				WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (1390789756,1473295742,2591732744,2600000780,3584424607,4217033850,
				     6190127738,12907948806,19737891830,60035602317,63587118302,77207319720,84556730791,87189232753)
                THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PF'

		        WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO IN ('SÓCIO PF','SÓCIO_PF','SOCIO_PF','SOCIO_PF_ABERTO','SOCIO_PF_ABERTU','SOCIO PF',
				                      'SOCIO-PF','SOCIO_PF E SOCI','PF') THEN 'SOCIO PF'
                WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO IN ('SOCIO_PJ','SOCIO_PJ_AB','SOCIO_PJ_ABERTO','SOCIO_PJ_FECHADO','SOCIO_PJ_FECH',
                                      'SOCIO_PJ_FECHAD','FUNDO_ABERTO','FUNDO_FECHADO','SOCIO-PJ','SOCIO_PJ[','PRINCIPAL QSA','SÓCIO_PJ','SOCIO ABERTO NA') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO IN ('SOCIO_ESTRANGEIRO','SOCIO_ESTRANG','SOCIO_ESTRANGEI','ESTRANGEIRO','SOCIO_PJ_ESTRAN','SOCIO_PJ_ESTRANGEIRO',
                                      'SOCIO ESTRANGEI','SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO','extrangeiro','socio estrageir')
                THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO'
				WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO = 'SOCIO_MENOR' THEN 'OUTRO SOCIO'
				WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO IN ('null','NULL') THEN 'S/INFO' ELSE t1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $24. AS TP_QUADRO,
		  t1.NuConsultado,
		  t1.NOME,
          T1.NuConsultado,
		  T1.docConsultado,
          t1.NOME_QUADRO,
          t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
          t6.CNAE,
           'ONBOARDING' AS NOM_BUREAUX,
          (CASE WHEN T1.DOCCONSULTADO IN (53616148000111,27760481000190,46194848000108) THEN 2135
                WHEN T1.DOCCONSULTADO = 54931530000182 THEN 2054
                WHEN T1.DOCCONSULTADO IN (39315196000149,52236187000120) THEN 2062
                ELSE t6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA END) AS COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
		  T1.DTH_CONSULTA

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA t1
	LEFT JOIN PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP_ND T6 ON (T6.NumCPFCNPJ = T1.DocConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BS_EXCLUIR_NOMES T2 ON (T1.NOME_QUADRO = T2.Name)
	WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('QUADRO_NAO_ABER','PRINCIPAL','IGOR MOUTINHO V','JOAO JUSTINO AC','ZULMAR ANTONIO',
                       'CESAR RODRIGUES','MANOEL RODRIGUE','MURILO PEIXOTO','RONALDO ALVES D') AND
          t1.DocConsultado NOT= t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO > 0 AND
          t1.NOME_QUADRO NOT IN ('NULL','','COTISTAS','PREOPERACIONAL','NÃO SE APLICA','SOCIOPF','SOCIOPJ','MENOR DE IDADE','S/INFO') AND
		  T1.DOCCONSULTADO NOT IN (1237611741,9034014797,10519066600,22636474811) AND T2.Name IS MISSING
	AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO IN (SELECT DOCCONSULTADO FROM BASE_TPPESSOA WHERE tipo NOT= 'F')

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  t1.docConsultado AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
		  (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
          (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo,
          (CASE WHEN T6.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T6.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME_QUADRO END) AS NOME_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN T7.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T7.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME END) AS NOME1

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t4 ON (t1.docConsultado = t4.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T6 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T6.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T7 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = T7.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	WHERE t1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('NULL','','COTISTAS','PREOPERACIONAL','NÃO SE APLICA','SOCIOPF','SOCIOPJ','MENOR DE IDADE','S/INFO')

;QUIT;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA;
DROP NOME NOME_QUADRO;
RENAME NOME1 = NOME;
RENAME NOME_QUADRO1 = NOME_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
           t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO > 0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) AND t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL > 0)
	LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) AND t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL > 0 )
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

;QUIT;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
			T1.*,
			(CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
                  ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
          	(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA = 2046 THEN 1
                                WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                         		WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                	     	    ELSE 0 END)> 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN(2054,2046) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     	 	(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &DISPENSADOS. THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Dispensados

      FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	  GROUP BY T1.NuConsultado

;QUIT;



 PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		 (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			   WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
	    (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
              WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
              WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			  WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
              ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

     FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA t1

 ;QUIT;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('S/INFO','PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Abertura,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_DiretoriaSa

    FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA T1
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA_CLASS AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.Nuconsultado,
		  (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'GRUPO_BV'
                WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'ABERTO - DISPENSADO'
                WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_SA'
                WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 'FALTA DIRETORIA SA'
                WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_1QSA'
                WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 'ADM PUBLICA'
                WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
                ELSE 'FALTA_ABERTURA' END) FORMAT $50. AS FL_QSA,
          (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 0
                WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 1
                WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 2
                WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 3
                WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 10
                WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 12
                WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 13
                ELSE 14 END)AS FL

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA t1
	GROUP BY T1.Nuconsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA_CLASS; BY Nuconsultado FL;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA_CLASS NODUPKEY; BY Nuconsultado;RUN;
PROC FREQ DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  0 AS Participacao,
	  t2.FL_QSA

    FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA T1
	LEFT JOIN ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA_CLASS t2 ON (t1.Nuconsultado = t2.Nuconsultado)

       	;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO_SA','ABERTO - DISPENSADO','GRUPO_BV','ABERTO_1QSA') then 0
	        ELSE 0 END) AS fl_risco_abertura

    FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA T1

       	;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA  t1
      LEFT JOIN BS_QSA_FIM t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN t2.DocConsultado is NOT missing AND T1.DTH_CONSULTA <=  T2.DTH_CONSULTA THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 0
	  ;

QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_QSA_FIM AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM BS_QSA_FIM t1
      LEFT JOIN ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE t2.DocConsultado IS MISSING
	  ;

QUIT;

DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
FORMAT TP_QUADRO $24.;
FORMAT NOME_QUADRO $200.;
FORMAT NOME $200.;
FORMAT FL_QSA $50.;
FORMAT NOM_BUREAUX $24.;
FORMAT DESCRICAO_QSA $100.;
SET BS_QSA_FIM ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_NOME AS
	SELECT
    *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_QSA_FIM
GROUP BY NuConsultado,DOC_NUM_QUADRO,NOME,NOME_QUADRO,FL_QSA,DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
HAVING Count(*) > 1
;
QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_AJUSTADA NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;



 

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  length(strip(put(T1.doc_num_quadro, best32.))) as tam

      FROM PLD.ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB  t1
      WHERE T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT = . AND TP_QUADRO NOT = 'PRINCIPAL' ;
QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_; RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1
      INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.NuConsultado,(MAX(t1.DATA_ENTRADA)) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DATA_ENTRADA,(MAX(t1.DtProcessamento)) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DtProcessamento
      				FROM WORK.ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1
      				GROUP BY t1.NuConsultado) T2 ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DATA_ENTRADA = T2.DATA_ENTRADA AND T1.DtProcessamento = T2.DtProcessamento)
;
QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_; RUN;



DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
	NOME = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME);
	NOME= prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME);
	NOME = upcase(NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = strip(NOME);
;RUN;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO= prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = upcase(NOME_QUADRO);
	NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = strip(NOME_QUADRO);
;RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
          (CASE WHEN T1.doc_num_quadro = 0  THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO'
                WHEN length(strip(put(T1.doc_num_quadro, best32.))) > 11 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                ELSE '' END) FORMAT $150. AS TP_QUADRO,
		  t1.NuConsultado,
		  t1.NOME,
          INPUT(T1.NuConsultado,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS docConsultado,
          t1.NOME_QUADRO,
          t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
          (CASE WHEN NUCONSULTADO = '50399553000137' THEN 5221400 ELSE T1.CNAE END) AS CNAE,
          'ONBOARDING' AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  T1.DATA_ENTRADA FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1

;QUIT;





DATA BASE_TIPO BASE_SEM;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
IF TP_QUADRO = '' THEN OUTPUT BASE_SEM;
ELSE OUTPUT BASE_TIPO;
RUN;

DATA BASE_TPPESSOA;
SET BASE_SEM;
        tipo = 'J';
		length cpf_val $11;
		cpf_val = put(doc_num_quadro, z11.);
		SOMA1 = (SUBSTR(cpf_val, 1,1)*10 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 2,1)*9 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 3,1)*8 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 4,1)*7 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 5,1)*6 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 6,1)*5 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 7,1)*4 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,8,1)*3 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,9,1)*2)*10;
		 	if mod(SOMA1,11)=10 then
				RESTO1 = 0;
			else
				RESTO1 = mod(SOMA1,11);

		SOMA2 = (SUBSTR(cpf_val, 1,1)*11 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 2,1)*10 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 3,1)*9 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 4,1)*8 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 5,1)*7 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 6,1)*6 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 7,1)*5 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,8,1)*4 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,9,1)*3
						+ SUBSTR(cpf_val,10,1)*2)*10;

            if mod(SOMA2,11)=10 then
				RESTO2 = 0;
			else
				RESTO2 = mod(SOMA2,11);

		dig1=(SUBSTR(cpf_val, 10,1));
        dig2=(SUBSTR(cpf_val, 11,1));
		    IF(dig1= RESTO1) AND (dig2=RESTO2)	THEN tipo = 'F';

	DROP SOMA1 SOMA2 dig1 dig2;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BASE_TPPESSOA AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
          (CASE WHEN T1.tipo = 'F' THEN 'SOCIO PF'
                ELSE 'SOCIO PJ' END) FORMAT $150. AS TP_QUADRO,
		  t1.NuConsultado,
		  TRIM(LEFT(UPPER(COMPRESS(T1.NOME, , 'kasw')))) format $200. AS NOME,
          INPUT(T1.NuConsultado,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS docConsultado,
          TRIM(LEFT(UPPER(COMPRESS(T1.NOME_QUADRO, , 'kasw')))) format $200. AS NOME_QUADRO,
          t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
          T1.CNAE,
          'ONBOARDING' AS NOM_BUREAUX

	FROM BASE_TPPESSOA t1

;QUIT;

PROC FREQ DATA=BASE_TPPESSOA ; TABLES TP_QUADRO ; RUN ;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
SET BASE_TIPO BASE_TPPESSOA;
RUN;

PROC FREQ DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB ; TABLES TP_QUADRO ; RUN ;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  t1.docConsultado AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
		  (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
          (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1
   	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t4 ON (t1.docConsultado = t4.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO> 0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) AND t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL>0)
	LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO >0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) AND t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL>0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

;QUIT;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
			T1.*,
			(CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
                  ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
           (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE  WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                         		WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                	     	    ELSE 0 END)> 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 		0 AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     		0 AS Fl_Dispensados

      FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	  GROUP BY T1.NuConsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

    FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB T1

;QUIT;

DATA ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
SET ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_CIB_CLASS AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
           T1.Nuconsultado,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'GRUPO_BV'
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'ABERTO - DISPENSADO'
				 ELSE 'ABERTO' END) FORMAT $50. AS FL_QSA,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 0
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 1
				 ELSE 3 END)AS FL

	FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1
             ;
QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_CIB_CLASS; BY Nuconsultado FL;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=ONBOARDING_CIB_CLASS NODUPKEY; BY Nuconsultado;RUN;
PROC FREQ DATA=ONBOARDING_CIB_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
       t1.*,
	   0 AS Fl_Abertura,
	   0 AS Fl_DiretoriaSa,
	   0 AS Participacao,
       t2.FL_QSA

      FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1
	  LEFT JOIN ONBOARDING_CIB_CLASS T2 ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO)
	  ;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO_SA','ABERTO - DISPENSADO','GRUPO_BV','ABERTO_1QSA') then 0
	        ELSE 0 END) AS fl_risco_abertura

    FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t1

       	;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB  t1
      LEFT JOIN BS_QSA_FIM t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN t2.DocConsultado is NOT missing AND T1.DTH_CONSULTA <=  T2.DTH_CONSULTA THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 0

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_QSA_FIM AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM BS_QSA_FIM t1
      LEFT JOIN ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE t2.DocConsultado IS MISSING
	  ;

QUIT;

DATA BS_QSA_FIM (WHERE=(docConsultado NOT = .));
SET BS_QSA_FIM ONBOARDING_QSA_CIB;
DROP NUM_QUADRO;
RUN;

DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
SET BS_QSA_FIM;
IF DOCCONSULTADO IN (10662547000106,35224835000100) AND CNAE IN (0,.) THEN DELETE;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BS_QSA_FIM NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
SET BS_QSA_FIM;
IF NUCONSULTADO IN ('000009.8132E16') THEN DELETE;
RUN;

DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
SET BS_QSA_FIM;
IF cnae IN (204000) THEN DELETE;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_NOME AS
	SELECT
    *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_QSA_FIM
GROUP BY NuConsultado,DOC_NUM_QUADRO,NOME,NOME_QUADRO,NOM_BUREAUX,DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
HAVING Count(*) > 1 AND DOCCONSULTADO > 0
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_FLAG AS
	SELECT DISTINCT
    FL_QSA,NOM_BUREAUX,NuConsultado

FROM BS_QSA_FIM
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_FLAG AS
	SELECT DISTINCT
    *,Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_COUNT_FLAG
GROUP BY NuConsultado
HAVING Count(*) > 1
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CONNECT TO SYBASE AS DBCON (
      SERVER=MORSYBCTR
      DATABASE=DBCOR
      USER=&USE_MORSYBCTR.
      PASSWORD=&SENHA_MORSYBCTR.
   );
CREATE TABLE dadosogtcr AS
	SELECT *
	FROM CONNECTION TO DBCON
	(SELECT DISTINCT
       TBP.CdPessoa,
       TBP.NmPessoa,
       TBP.NuCpfCnpj,
       r.NmRepresentanteLegal,
       r.NuCpf,
       r.DtUltimaAtualizacao,
       s.PcParticipacao
from TbRepresentanteLegal r
inner join TbSocio s on r.CdParceiroComercial = s.CdParceiroComercial and r.SqRepresentanteLegal = s.SqRepresentanteLegal
inner join DBCOR.dbo.TbParceiroComercial TPC ON TPC.CdParceiroComercial = s.CdParceiroComercial
INNER JOIN DBCOR.dbo.TbPessoa TBP ON TPC.CdPessoa = TBP.CdPessoa
where 0=0
and r.FlAtivo = 'S'
order by r.cdparceirocomercial desc
);
	DISCONNECT FROM DBCON;
	quit;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          compress(T1.NuCpfCnpj, '.,/-') AS NUCONSULTADO,
		  INPUT(compress(T1.NuCpfCnpj, '.,/-'),BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOCCONSULTADO,
		  INPUT(compress(T1.NuCpfCnpj, '.,/-'),BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  T1.NmPessoa AS NOME,
	      INPUT(T1.NuCpf,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
		  T1.NmRepresentanteLegal AS NOME_QUADRO,
		  T1.PcParticipacao AS PartQuadro,
		  (CASE WHEN LENGTH(COMPRESS(T1.NuCpf)) = 14 THEN 'PRINCIPAL QSA'
                WHEN LENGTH(COMPRESS(T1.NuCpf)) = 11 THEN 'SOCIO PF' ELSE '' END) AS TP_QUADRO,
		  'GCTR' AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  DATEPART(T1.DtUltimaAtualizacao) FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM dadosogtcr t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_EXCLUIR_NOMES T3 ON (upcase(T1.NmRepresentanteLegal) = T3.NAME)
	  where T3.NAME IS MISSING 

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   	CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
   		SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*

		FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1 INNER JOIN
             (SELECT DISTINCT t1.docConsultado, (MAX(t1.DTH_CONSULTA)) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA
                     FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1  GROUP BY t1.docConsultado) T2
        ON (T1.DTH_CONSULTA = T2.DTH_CONSULTA AND T1.docConsultado = T2.docConsultado)
        
;QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
SET BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = upcase(NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = strip(NOME_QUADRO);
;RUN;

DATA BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
SET BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
    NOME = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = upcase(NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = strip(NOME);
;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN T2.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T2.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME_QUADRO END) AS NOME_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN T4.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T4.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME END) AS NOME1

	FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t3 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t3.DOC_NUM)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.))
	LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T2 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T4 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = T4.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)

;QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
SET BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
DROP NOME_QUADRO NOME;
RENAME NOME_QUADRO1 = NOME_QUADRO;
RENAME NOME1 = NOME;
RUN;


PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

	FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM and T1.DOCCONSULTADO > 0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) and t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL >0)
	LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO>0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) and t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL>0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t6.CNAE,
      t6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
       (CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
             ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
	  (CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA = 2046 THEN 1
            WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
            WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
            ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN(2054,2046) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &DISPENSADOS. THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Dispensados,
	 (CASE WHEN t3.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
	 (CASE WHEN t3.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
	 (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
     (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo

    FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR T1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t3 ON (t1.docConsultado = t3.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP_ND t6 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t6.NUMCPFCNPJ)
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

    FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR T1

;QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
SET BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
            T1.NUCONSULTADO,
			T1.NOME,
			t1.NOM_BUREAUX,
            (SUM(t1.PartQuadro)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao

      FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1
	  WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('DIRETORIA','PRINCIPAL QSA')
      GROUP BY T1.NUCONSULTADO,
               t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          COALESCE(T2.Participacao,0) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao
      FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR T1
      LEFT JOIN PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA t2 ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO)
      ;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PF'
			    WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T4.DOCCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO PJ' ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

      FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.NUCONSULTADO,t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
                 FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR T1) T3
			ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T3.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL)
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT T1.NUCONSULTADO,t1.DOCCONSULTADO FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1
                 WHERE t1.DOCCONSULTADO NOT IN (.,0,3,11)) T4
			ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T4.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T4.DOCCONSULTADO)

;QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
SET BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('S/INFO','PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Abertura,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_DiretoriaSa

    FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR T1
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR_CLASS AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.Nuconsultado,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'GRUPO_BV'
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'ABERTO - DISPENSADO'
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_SA'
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 'FALTA DIRETORIA SA'
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_1QSA'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 'ADM PUBLICA'
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 ELSE 'FALTA_ABERTURA' END) FORMAT $50. AS FL_QSA,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 0
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 1
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 2
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 3
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 4
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 10
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 12
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 13
				 ELSE 14 END)AS FL

	FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR t1
	GROUP BY T1.Nuconsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=BS_SOCIO_GCTR_CLASS; BY Nuconsultado FL;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=BS_SOCIO_GCTR_CLASS NODUPKEY; BY Nuconsultado;RUN;
PROC FREQ DATA=BS_SOCIO_GCTR_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t2.FL_QSA

    FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR T1
	LEFT JOIN BS_SOCIO_GCTR_CLASS t2 ON (t1.Nuconsultado = t2.Nuconsultado)

       	;
QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
SET BS_SOCIO_GCTR;
DROP PartQuadro;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BS_SOCIO_GCTR NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	   (CASE WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO_SA','ABERTO - DISPENSADO','GRUPO_BV','ABERTO_1QSA') then 0
	         WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO') and t1.Participacao = 0 then 2
	         WHEN t1.Participacao >= 100 then 0
			 WHEN t1.Participacao >= 75 then 1
		     ELSE 3 END) AS fl_risco_abertura

    FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR T1

       	;
QUIT;

PROC FREQ DATA=BS_SOCIO_GCTR ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_GCTR AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM BS_SOCIO_GCTR  t1
      LEFT JOIN BS_QSA_FIM t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN t2.DocConsultado is NOT missing AND T1.DTH_CONSULTA <=  T2.DTH_CONSULTA THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 0;

QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_QSA_FIM AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM BS_QSA_FIM t1
      LEFT JOIN BS_SOCIO_GCTR t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE t2.DocConsultado IS MISSING
	  ;

QUIT;




 



PROC SORT DATA= dadosonepldqsa_ren; BY NuCnpjEmresa DESCENDING DtInclusao;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA= dadosonepldqsa_ren OUT=dadosonepldqsa_ren_ND NODUPKEY;
BY NuCnpjEmresa NmRtrnoCadeiaSocietariaSerasa NuDocumento PcParticipacao;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_DATA_MAXIMA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
           t1.NuCnpjEmresa,
		   DATEPART((MAX(t1.DtInclusao))) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DtInclusao
      FROM WORK.DADOSONEPLDQSA_REN_ND t1
	  GROUP BY t1.NuCnpjEmresa;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_TOTAL_PARTICIPACAO_ONEPLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NuCnpjEmresa,
          T1.NuDocumento,
          t1.PcParticipacao,
          T2.DtInclusao AS DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM dadosonepldqsa_ren_ND t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_DATA_MAXIMA T2 ON (T1.NuCnpjEmresa = T2.NuCnpjEmresa AND T2.DtInclusao = DATEPART(t1.DtInclusao))
      LEFT JOIN dadosonepldqsa_ren T3 ON (t1.NuCnpjEmresa = T3.NuDocumento)
      WHERE T2.DtInclusao IS NOT MISSING 

;QUIT;

DATA dadosonepldqsa_ren_ND;
SET dadosonepldqsa_ren_ND;
IF SUBSTR(NuCnpjEmresa,1,11) = NuDocumento THEN DELETE;
RUN;



DATA ONEPLD_NOMES (KEEP=NmRtrnoCadeiaSocietariaSerasa NuDocumento DtInclusao);
SET dadosonepldqsa_ren_ND;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=ONEPLD_NOMES; BY NuDocumento DESCENDING DtInclusao;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=ONEPLD_NOMES nodupkey; BY NuDocumento;RUN;

PROC SQL;
 CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
 SELECT DISTINCT
          (CASE WHEN t1.NuCnpjEmresa = t1.NuDocumento THEN 'PRINCIPAL'
               	WHEN t1.NuDocumento = '00000000000' THEN 'SOCIO_ESTRANGEIRO'
				WHEN t1.NuDocumento = '99999999900' THEN 'SOCIO_MENOR'
				ELSE '' END) FORMAT $50. AS TP_QUADRO,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.NuCnpjEmresa = '00301893000195' THEN '03471344001149' ELSE t1.NuCnpjEmresa END) AS NuConsultado,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.NuCnpjEmresa = '00301893000195' THEN 3471344001149 ELSE INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) END) FORMAT BEST14. AS docConsultado,
		  (CASE WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) = 8713340000171	THEN 'MASTER COIN FACTORING LTDA'
                WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) = 23432773000142 THEN 'PRT - PNEUS E RODAS TECNICAS LTDA'
                WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) = 38484625000149 THEN 'JD SERVICOS ESTETICOS LTDA'
                WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) = 41094306000131 THEN 'PEREIRA ECA VEICULOS LTDA'
                WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) = 46022632000157 THEN 'JULIANA SABBATINE ESTETICA AVANCADA LTDA'
                WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) = 48401008000196 THEN 'SINERGIA ECO SOLAR E SOLUCOES INTELIGENTES LTDA'
                WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.) = 76543115000194 THEN 'BANCO SISTEMA SA'
				WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,BEST14.) = 0 THEN T1.NmRtrnoCadeiaSocietariaSerasa
                ELSE T2.NmRtrnoCadeiaSocietariaSerasa END) FORMAT $200. AS NOME,
		  INPUT(T1.NuDocumento,14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
		  t1.NuDocumento AS NUM_QUADRO,
		  (CASE WHEN INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,BEST14.) = 0 THEN T1.NmRtrnoCadeiaSocietariaSerasa
                ELSE T4.NmRtrnoCadeiaSocietariaSerasa END) FORMAT $200. AS NOME_QUADRO,
		  'ONEPLD' AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  T3.DTH_CONSULTA,
          T3.PcParticipacao

    FROM dadosonepldqsa_ren_ND T1
   	LEFT JOIN dadosonepldqsa_ren_ND T2 ON (T1.NuCnpjEmresa = T2.NuDocumento)
	INNER JOIN BS_TOTAL_PARTICIPACAO_ONEPLD T3 ON (T1.NuCnpjEmresa = T3.NuCnpjEmresa AND T1.NuDocumento = T3.NuDocumento)
	LEFT JOIN ONEPLD_NOMES T4 ON (T1.NuDocumento = T4.NuDocumento)
	WHERE INPUT(T1.NuCnpjEmresa,14.)  NOT IN (415256208,415256208,866134726)
  ;

QUIT;





DATA BASE_TIPO BASE_SEM;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
IF TP_QUADRO = '' THEN OUTPUT BASE_SEM;
ELSE OUTPUT BASE_TIPO;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BASE_SEM NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

DATA BASE_TPPESSOA;
SET BASE_SEM;
        tipo = 'J';
		length cpf_val $11;
		cpf_val = put(DOC_NUM_QUADRO, z11.);
		SOMA1 = (SUBSTR(cpf_val, 1,1)*10 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 2,1)*9 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 3,1)*8 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 4,1)*7 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 5,1)*6 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 6,1)*5 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 7,1)*4 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,8,1)*3 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,9,1)*2)*10;
		 	if mod(SOMA1,11)=10 then
				RESTO1 = 0;
			else
				RESTO1 = mod(SOMA1,11);

		SOMA2 = (SUBSTR(cpf_val, 1,1)*11 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 2,1)*10 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 3,1)*9 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 4,1)*8 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 5,1)*7 + SUBSTR(cpf_val, 6,1)*6 +
      			 SUBSTR(cpf_val, 7,1)*5 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,8,1)*4 + SUBSTR(cpf_val,9,1)*3
						+ SUBSTR(cpf_val,10,1)*2)*10;

            if mod(SOMA2,11)=10 then
				RESTO2 = 0;
			else
				RESTO2 = mod(SOMA2,11);

		dig1=(SUBSTR(cpf_val, 10,1));
        dig2=(SUBSTR(cpf_val, 11,1));
		    IF(dig1= RESTO1) AND (dig2=RESTO2)	THEN tipo = 'F';

	DROP SOMA1 SOMA2 dig1 dig2;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
 CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
 SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
          (CASE WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T1.TP_QUADRO
                WHEN tipo = 'J' THEN 'PRINCIPAL QSA'
                ELSE 'SOCIO PF' END) FORMAT $50. AS TP_QUADRO1

    FROM QSA_ONE_PLD T1
   	LEFT JOIN BASE_TPPESSOA T2 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
  ;

QUIT;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = upcase(NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = strip(NOME_QUADRO);
;RUN;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
    NOME = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = upcase(NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = strip(NOME);
;RUN;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD (WHERE=(TP_QUADRO NOT= 'PRINCIPAL'));
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
IF INPUT(SUBSTR(NuConsultado,1,11),BEST14.) = DOC_NUM_QUADRO THEN DELETE;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN T4.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T4.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME_QUADRO END) AS NOME_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN T3.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T3.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME END) AS NOME1

      FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.NUCONSULTADO,t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO
                FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1 WHERE t1.TP_QUADRO = 'SOCIO PF') T2
					ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	  LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T4.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	  LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T3 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' AND T2.NUCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) =0

;QUIT;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
DROP NOME_QUADRO NOME;
RENAME NOME1 = NOME;
RENAME NOME_QUADRO1 = NOME_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  t1.docConsultado AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
		  (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
          (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo

	FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1
 	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t4 ON (t1.docConsultado = t4.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

      FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM and T1.DOCCONSULTADO > 0)
      LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) and t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL > 0)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO > 0)
      LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) and t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL >0)
	  LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	  LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

 ;QUIT;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
			T1.*,
			(CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
             ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
          	(CASE WHEN DocConsultado IN (73957000168) THEN 3512300
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (5423963000111) THEN 6120501
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (7162848000166) THEN 8533300
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (9011730000162) THEN 8220200
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (9284610000139) THEN 4110700
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (9406163000143, 10813031000115, 15816139000185, 16749597000101,
                                     17040053000139, 18805011000104, 28439104000116) THEN 6462000
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (13673743000174) THEN 6319400
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (18777673000118, 21118742000196) THEN 6463800
                  WHEN DocConsultado IN (58687902000128) THEN 6619399 ELSE t6.CNAE END) AS CNAE,
      	   	t6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
	 		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA = 2046 THEN 1
                                WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                         		WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                	     	    ELSE 0 END)> 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN(2054,2046) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     	 	(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &DISPENSADOS. THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Dispensados

      FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	  LEFT JOIN PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP_ND T6 ON (T6.NumCPFCNPJ = T1.DocConsultado)
	  GROUP BY T1.NuConsultado

;QUIT;



 PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		 (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			   WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

     FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1

 ;QUIT;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_ONEPLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NUCONSULTADO,
		  t1.NOME,
		  T1.NOM_BUREAUX,
		  (SUM(t1.PcParticipacao)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao

      FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1
	  WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('DIRETORIA','PRINCIPAL QSA')
      GROUP BY t1.NUCONSULTADO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          (case when T2.Participacao is missing then 0 else T2.Participacao end) as Participacao

      FROM QSA_ONE_PLD T1
      LEFT JOIN PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_ONEPLD t2 ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO)
      ;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('S/INFO','PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Abertura,
	    (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_DiretoriaSa

    FROM QSA_ONE_PLD T1
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD_CLASS AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.Nuconsultado,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'GRUPO_BV'
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'ABERTO - DISPENSADO'
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_SA'
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 'FALTA DIRETORIA SA'
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_1QSA'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 'ADM PUBLICA'
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 ELSE 'FALTA_ABERTURA' END) FORMAT $50. AS FL_QSA,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 0
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 1
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 2
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 3
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 4
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 10
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 12
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 13
				 ELSE 14 END)AS FL

	FROM QSA_ONE_PLD t1
	GROUP BY T1.Nuconsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=QSA_ONE_PLD_CLASS; BY Nuconsultado FL;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=QSA_ONE_PLD_CLASS NODUPKEY; BY Nuconsultado;RUN;
PROC FREQ DATA=QSA_ONE_PLD_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t2.FL_QSA

    FROM QSA_ONE_PLD T1
	LEFT JOIN QSA_ONE_PLD_CLASS t2 ON (t1.Nuconsultado = t2.Nuconsultado)

       	;
QUIT;

DATA QSA_ONE_PLD;
SET QSA_ONE_PLD;
DROP NUM_QUADRO;RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=QSA_ONE_PLD NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO_SA','ABERTO - DISPENSADO','GRUPO_BV','ABERTO_1QSA') then 0
	        WHEN t1.Participacao >= 100 then 0
			WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO') and t1.Participacao = 0 then 2
		    WHEN t1.Participacao >= 75 then 1
		    ELSE 3 END) AS fl_risco_abertura

    FROM QSA_ONE_PLD T1

       	;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_ONE_PLD AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM QSA_ONE_PLD  t1
      LEFT JOIN BS_QSA_FIM t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN t2.DocConsultado is NOT missing AND T1.DTH_CONSULTA <=  T2.DTH_CONSULTA THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 0
	  ;

QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_QSA_FIM AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM BS_QSA_FIM t1
      LEFT JOIN QSA_ONE_PLD t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE t2.DocConsultado IS MISSING
	  ;

QUIT;




 

DATA BS_SERASA_QSA (WHERE=(TP_QUADRO NOT = 'S/INFO' AND NOME_QUADRO NOT = 'null'));
SET PESQUISA_SERASA_GCP;
format tsinclusao date9.;
tsinclusao = input(substr(MaiorData, 1, 10), yymmdd10.);
run;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SERASA_QSA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
           t1.*

      FROM BS_SERASA_QSA t1
	  INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.NumCpfCnpj, (MAX(t1.tsinclusao)) FORMAT=DATETIME18. AS tsinclusao
      				FROM BS_SERASA_QSA t1
      				GROUP BY t1.NumCpfCnpj) T2 ON (T1.NumCpfCnpj = T2.NumCpfCnpj AND T1.tsinclusao = T2.tsinclusao)
     ;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_NOME_SERASA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NumCpfCnpj FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM,
          t1.NOME,
          LENGTH(T1.NOME) AS TAM

      FROM BS_SERASA_QSA T1

UNION ALL

SELECT DISTINCT
          INPUT(t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM,
          t1.NOME_QUADRO AS NOME,
          LENGTH(T1.NOME_QUADRO) AS TAM

      FROM WORK.BS_SERASA_QSA T1

     ;
QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA= BS_NOME_SERASA ; BY DOC_NUM DESCENDING TAM;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA= BS_NOME_SERASA NODUPKEY; BY DOC_NUM;RUN;

DATA BS_NOME_SERASA;
SET BS_NOME_SERASA;
	NOME = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME);
	NOME= prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME);
	NOME = upcase(NOME);
	NOME = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = strip(NOME);
;RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.NumCpfCnpj FORMAT BEST14. AS DocConsultado,
		  PUT(T1.NumCpfCnpj,Z14.) AS NuConsultado,
		  T3.NOME FORMAT $150. AS NOME,
		  t1.CNAE,
          t1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
          t1.STATUS as ST_RECEITA,
		  INPUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
		  (CASE WHEN T4.NOME IS MISSING THEN T1.NOME_QUADRO
                ELSE T4.NOME END) FORMAT $150. AS NOME_QUADRO,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO = 'SOCIO_PF' THEN 'SOCIO PF'
		        WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO = 'SOCIO_PJ' THEN 'SOCIO PJ' ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $24. AS TP_QUADRO,
		  'SERASA' FORMAT $24. AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  T1.tsinclusao FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA,
		  input(T1.capital_total, comma10.2)  as PartQuadro

	FROM BS_SERASA_QSA t1
	LEFT JOIN BS_NOME_SERASA T3 ON (T3.DOC_NUM = T1.NumCpfCnpj)
	LEFT JOIN BS_NOME_SERASA T4 ON (T4.DOC_NUM = INPUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,BEST14.))
 	WHERE T1.NOME_QUADRO NOT= 'null'
    GROUP BY T1.NumCpfCnpj

;QUIT;

 PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
             t1.*,
			(CASE WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 28614904 THEN 28614904000126
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 97540846 THEN 97540846000156
				  WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 42412000194 THEN 424129000127
				  WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 90017419000 THEN 90017419000149
                  WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 1010496 THEN 1010496000147
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 17078020 THEN 17078020000188
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 28614902 THEN 28614902000137
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 31941862 THEN 31941862000142
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 35034436 THEN 28632328000140
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 54603378 THEN 54603378000109
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 72807514 THEN 72807514000164
			      WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 88611835 THEN 88611835000129
                  else t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO END) format best14. as DOC_NUM_QUADRO1

FROM BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA T1;
QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA;
SET BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA;
DROP DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RENAME DOC_NUM_QUADRO1 = DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  t1.docConsultado AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
		  (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
          (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo

	FROM BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA t1
 	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t4 ON (t1.docConsultado = t4.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)

;QUIT;


PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

	FROM BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM and T1.DOCCONSULTADO>0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) and t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL>0)
	LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO >0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) and t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL>0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

;QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA;
SET BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
			T1.*,
			(CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
             ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
          	(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA = 2046 THEN 1
                                WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                         		WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                	     	    ELSE 0 END)> 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN(2054,2046) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     	 	(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &DISPENSADOS. THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Dispensados

      FROM BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	  GROUP BY T1.NuConsultado

;QUIT;



 PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		 (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			   WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

     FROM BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA t1

 ;QUIT;

DATA BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA;
SET BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_SERASA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NUCONSULTADO,
		  T1.NOME,
		  T1.NOM_BUREAUX,
		  (SUM(t1.PartQuadro)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao

      FROM WORK.BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA t1
	  WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('DIRETORIA','PRINCIPAL QSA')
      GROUP BY t1.NUCONSULTADO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_SOCIO_SERASA_AJUSTADA AS
