    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = strip(NOME_NIVEL);
;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (41491475749,503190942) THEN 'SOCIO PF'
		        WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 50220886000157 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Jurídica' THEN 'PRINCIPAL QSA'
                WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Física' AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 0 AND PartQuadro > 0 THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PF'
				WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Física' AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 0 AND PartQuadro = 0 THEN 'DIRETORIA ESTRANGEIRO'
				WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Física' AND T1.PartQuadro = 0 THEN 'DIRETORIA'
                ELSE 'SOCIO PF' END) FORMAT $24. AS TP_QUADRO,
		   (CASE WHEN t1.NOME_NIVEL CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA' THEN T1.DOCCONSULTADO ELSE T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL END) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL1,
		   (CASE WHEN t4.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
		   (CASE WHEN t4.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
		   (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
           (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo,
		   (CASE WHEN T2.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T2.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME_QUADRO END) AS NOME_QUADRO1,
		   (CASE WHEN t1.NOME_NIVEL CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA' AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO = (CASE WHEN t1.NOME_NIVEL CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA' THEN T1.DOCCONSULTADO ELSE T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL END)
                 THEN T1.NOME
                 WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T3.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME_NIVEL END) AS NOME_NIVEL1

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t4 ON (t1.docConsultado = t4.docConsultado)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)
	  LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T2 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	  LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T3 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL NOME_QUADRO NOME_NIVEL;
RENAME DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL1 = DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL;
RENAME NOME_QUADRO1 = NOME_QUADRO;
RENAME NOME_NIVEL1 = NOME_NIVEL;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PF'
			    WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T4.DOCCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO PJ' ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1,
	      (CASE WHEN t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (.) THEN 0 ELSE t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO END) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO1

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.NUCONSULTADO, t1.NOME_NIVEL,t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
                 FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES T1) T3
			ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T3.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
                 AND T1.NOME_QUADRO = T3.NOME_NIVEL)
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT T1.NUCONSULTADO,t1.DOCCONSULTADO FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
                 WHERE t1.DOCCONSULTADO NOT IN (.,0,3,11)) T4
			ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T4.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T4.DOCCONSULTADO)

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DOC_NUM_QUADRO1 = DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_EXCLUIR_NOMES T3 ON (T1.NOME_QUADRO = T3.NAME)
	  where T3.NAME IS MISSING

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES (WHERE=(DOCCONSULTADO>1));
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP TIPO_PESSOA__C ID_QUADRO ID_PRINCIPAL;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

	FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO > 0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) AND t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL > 0)
	LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO > 0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) AND T5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL >0 )
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t3.DOCCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN T3.TP_QUADRO
		        ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN t3.DOCCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN T3.DESCRICAO_QSA
		        ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA1

	FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT t1.DOCCONSULTADO,t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,t1.NOME_QUADRO,t1.TP_QUADRO,T1.DESCRICAO_QSA FROM WORK.PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
               WHERE t1.TP_QUADRO IN ('SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PJ', 'SOCIO PJ')) t3
               ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t3.DOCCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO
                   AND T1.NOME_QUADRO = T3.NOME_QUADRO)

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t6.CNAE,
      t6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
       (CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
             ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
	  (CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA = 2046 THEN 1
            WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
            WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
            ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN(2054,2046) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     
     (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &DISPENSADOS. THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Dispensados
	 

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES T1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	LEFT JOIN PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP_ND t6 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t6.NUMCPFCNPJ)
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES T1

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYC AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NUCONSULTADO,
		  t1.NOME,
          t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  T1.NOME_NIVEL,
          (SUM(t1.PartQuadro)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao,
		  (CASE WHEN INPUT(t1.NUCONSULTADO,BEST14.) = t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS FLAG,
		  T1.NOM_BUREAUX

      FROM WORK.PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
	  WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('DIRETORIA','PRINCIPAL QSA')
      GROUP BY t1.NUCONSULTADO,
               t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
               T1.NOME_NIVEL;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYC AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
           t1.NUCONSULTADO,
		   t1.nome,
		   t1.NOM_BUREAUX,
          

FLOOR((MEAN(t1.Participacao))) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao
      FROM WORK.PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYC t1
      GROUP BY t1.NUCONSULTADO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          (case when T2.Participacao is missing then 0 else T2.Participacao end) as Participacao
      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES T1
      LEFT JOIN PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYC t2 ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO)
      ;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          (CASE WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' AND t1.Participacao > 100 and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO > 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' AND t1.Participacao > 100 and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (0,.) THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PJ'
				ELSE t1.TP_QUADRO END) format $100. as TP_QUADRO1

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('S/INFO','PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Abertura,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_DiretoriaSa

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES T1
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES_CLASS AS
   SELECT DISTINCT

          T1.Nuconsultado,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'GRUPO_BV'
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'ABERTO - DISPENSADO'
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_SA'
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 'FALTA DIRETORIA SA'
				 WHEN T1.Participacao > 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_1QSA'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 'ADM PUBLICA'
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 ELSE 'FALTA_ABERTURA' END) FORMAT $50. AS FL_QSA,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 0
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 1
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 2
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 3
				 WHEN T1.Participacao > 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 4
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 10
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 12
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 13
				 ELSE 14 END)AS FL

	FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
	GROUP BY T1.Nuconsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES_CLASS; BY Nuconsultado FL;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES_CLASS NODUPKEY; BY Nuconsultado;RUN;
PROC FREQ DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t2.FL_QSA

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES T1
	LEFT JOIN PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES_CLASS t2 ON (t1.Nuconsultado = t2.Nuconsultado)

       	;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO_SA','ABERTO - DISPENSADO','GRUPO_BV','ABERTO_1QSA') then 0
            WHEN t1.Participacao >= 100 then 0
			WHEN t1.Participacao >= 75 then 1
			ELSE 3 END) AS fl_risco_abertura

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1

       	;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.NUCONSULTADO,t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO
                FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1 WHERE t1.TP_QUADRO = 'DIRETORIA') T2
					ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO = 'SOCIO PF' AND T2.NUCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) =0

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES  t1
      LEFT JOIN PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN t2.DocConsultado is NOT missing AND T1.DTH_CONSULTA <=  T2.DTH_CONSULTA THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 0;

QUIT;



 

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
DROP PartQuadro;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES;
IF NOME = 'LILIANE MILAGRES' THEN DELETE; RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT AS
	SELECT
    *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES
GROUP BY Nuconsultado,FL_QSA,DOC_NUM_QUADRO,DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
HAVING Count(*) > 1 and DOC_NUM_QUADRO not in (99999999999999,0,.) and DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL not in (99999999999999,0,.)
;
QUIT;

PROC FREQ DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;





DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
FORMAT TP_QUADRO $24.;
FORMAT NOME_QUADRO $200.;
FORMAT NOME $200.;
FORMAT FL_QSA $50.;
FORMAT NOM_BUREAUX $24.;
FORMAT DESCRICAO_QSA $100.;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES ;

RUN;



PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_NOME AS
	SELECT
    *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_QSA_FIM
GROUP BY NuConsultado,DOC_NUM_QUADRO,NOME,NOME_QUADRO,DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
HAVING Count(*) > 1 AND DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (0,.) AND DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL NOT IN (0,.)
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_FLAG AS
	SELECT DISTINCT
    FL_QSA,NOM_BUREAUX,NuConsultado

FROM BS_QSA_FIM
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_FLAG AS
	SELECT DISTINCT
    *,Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_COUNT_FLAG
GROUP BY NuConsultado
HAVING Count(*) > 1
;
QUIT;




 

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.ID AS ID_QUADRO,
		  t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C AS ID_PRINCIPAL,
		  compress(T2.'CPF/CNPJ'N, '.,/-') AS NUCONSULTADO,
		  INPUT(compress(T2.'CPF/CNPJ'N, '.,/-'),BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOCCONSULTADO,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ IS NOT MISSING THEN T4.CPF_CNPJ__C
                ELSE T1.CNPJ_ASSOCIADO_AUXILIAR__C END ) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ IS NOT MISSING THEN T4.NAME
                ELSE T2.Empresa END ) AS NOME_NIVEL,
		  T2.Empresa AS NOME,
	      T1.'CPF_CNPJ__C'N FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
		  T1.NAME AS NOME_QUADRO,
		  T1.PARTICIPACAO__C AS PartQuadro,
		  T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C,
		  'SALESFORCE' AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  T2.'Data da aprovação'n FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM extractKYP t1
	  LEFT JOIN Relatorio_ID_KYP t2 ON ((substr(t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C, 1, length(t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C) - 3)) = t2.'KYP: ID'n )
	  LEFT JOIN BS_EXCLUIR_NOMES T3 ON (upcase(T1.NAME) = T3.NAME)
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.ID,t1.NAME,t1.CPF_CNPJ__C,t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C
                 FROM extractKYP t1) T4 ON (t4.ID = T1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ AND T4.KYP_AUXILIAR__C = T1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C)
	  where t2.Status IN ('Aprovado PLD','ativo') AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA'
       AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT CONTAINS 'COTISTA' AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT = 'OUTROS PARTICIPANTES COM PARTICIPAO INDIVIDUAL INFERIOR A'
       AND T3.NAME IS MISSING

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_2 AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.ID AS ID_QUADRO,
		  t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C AS ID_PRINCIPAL,
		  compress(T2.'CPF/CNPJ'N, '.,/-') AS NUCONSULTADO,
		  INPUT(compress(T2.'CPF/CNPJ'N, '.,/-'),BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOCCONSULTADO,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ IS NOT MISSING THEN INPUT(T4.CPF_CNPJ__C,BEST14.)
                ELSE INPUT(T1.CNPJ_ASSOCIADO_AUXILIAR__C, BEST14.) END) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ IS NOT MISSING THEN T4.NAME
                ELSE T2.Empresa END ) AS NOME_NIVEL,
		  T2.Empresa AS NOME,
	      INPUT(T1.'CPF_CNPJ__C'N,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
		  T1.NAME AS NOME_QUADRO,
		  T1.PARTICIPACAO__C AS PartQuadro,
		  T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C,
		  'SALESFORCE' AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  T2.'Data da aprovação'n FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM WORK.'AnaliseAcionistasKYP24.06'n t1
	  LEFT JOIN Relatorio_ID_KYP t2 ON ((substr(t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C, 1, length(t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C) - 3)) = t2.'KYP: ID'n )
	  LEFT JOIN BS_EXCLUIR_NOMES T3 ON (upcase(T1.NAME) = T3.NAME)
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.ID,t1.NAME,t1.CPF_CNPJ__C,t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C
                 FROM WORK.'AnaliseAcionistasKYP24.06'n t1) T4 ON (t4.ID = T1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ AND T4.KYP_AUXILIAR__C = T1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C)
	  where t2.Status IN ('Aprovado PLD','ativo') AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA'
       AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT CONTAINS 'COTISTA' AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT = 'OUTROS PARTICIPANTES COM PARTICIPAO INDIVIDUAL INFERIOR A'
       AND T3.NAME IS MISSING

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_INI AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.ID AS ID_QUADRO,
		  t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C AS ID_PRINCIPAL,
		  compress(T2.'CPF/CNPJ'N, '.,/-') AS NUCONSULTADO,
		  INPUT(compress(T2.'CPF/CNPJ'N, '.,/-'),BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOCCONSULTADO,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ IS NOT MISSING THEN T4.CPF_CNPJ__C
                ELSE INPUT(compress(T1.CNPJ_ASSOCIADO_AUXILIAR__C, '.,/-'),BEST14.) END ) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ IS NOT MISSING THEN T4.NAME
                ELSE T2.Empresa END ) AS NOME_NIVEL,
		  T2.Empresa AS NOME,
	      T1.'CPF_CNPJ__C'N FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
		  T1.NAME AS NOME_QUADRO,
		  T1.PARTICIPACAO__C AS PartQuadro,
		  T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C,
		  'SALESFORCE' AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  T2.'Data da aprovação'n FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA

      FROM extractKYP_INI t1
	  LEFT JOIN Relatorio_ID_KYP t2 ON ((substr(t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C, 1, length(t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C) - 3)) = t2.'KYP: ID'n )
	  LEFT JOIN BS_EXCLUIR_NOMES T3 ON (upcase(T1.NAME) = T3.NAME)
	  LEFT JOIN PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES T5 ON (t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C = t5.ID_PRINCIPAL)
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.ID,t1.NAME,t1.CPF_CNPJ__C,t1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C
                 FROM extractKYP_INI t1) T4 ON (t4.ID = T1.ANALISE_DE_ACIONISTA_ASSOCIADA__ AND T4.KYP_AUXILIAR__C = T1.KYP_AUXILIAR__C)
	  where t2.Status IN ('Aprovado PLD','ativo') AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA'
       AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT CONTAINS 'COTISTA' AND UPCASE(T1.NAME) NOT = 'OUTROS PARTICIPANTES COM PARTICIPAO INDIVIDUAL INFERIOR A'
       AND T3.NAME IS MISSING AND t5.ID_PRINCIPAL IS MISSING

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES ;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_INI PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_2;
RUN;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = upcase(NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = strip(NOME_QUADRO);
;RUN;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
    NOME = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = upcase(NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = strip(NOME);
;RUN;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = upcase(NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_NIVEL);
    NOME_NIVEL = strip(NOME_NIVEL);
;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (41491475749,503190942) THEN 'SOCIO PF'
		        WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 50220886000157 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Jurídica' THEN 'PRINCIPAL QSA'
                WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Física' AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 0 AND PartQuadro > 0 THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PF'
				WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Física' AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 0 AND PartQuadro = 0 THEN 'DIRETORIA ESTRANGEIRO'
				WHEN T1.TIPO_PESSOA__C = 'Física' AND T1.PartQuadro = 0 THEN 'DIRETORIA'
                ELSE 'SOCIO PF' END) FORMAT $24. AS TP_QUADRO,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.NOME_NIVEL CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA' THEN T1.DOCCONSULTADO ELSE T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL END) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL1,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
		  (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
          (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo,
		  (CASE WHEN T2.NOME_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T2.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME_QUADRO END) AS NOME_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.NOME_NIVEL CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA' AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO = (CASE WHEN t1.NOME_NIVEL CONTAINS 'DIRETORIA' THEN T1.DOCCONSULTADO ELSE T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL END)
                 THEN T1.NOME
                 WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IS NOT MISSING THEN T3.NOME_QUADRO ELSE T1.NOME_NIVEL END) AS NOME_NIVEL1

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t4 ON (t1.docConsultado = t4.docConsultado)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)
	  LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T2 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)
	  LEFT JOIN NOMES_CORRETOS T3 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL NOME_QUADRO NOME_NIVEL;
RENAME DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL1 = DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL;
RENAME NOME_QUADRO1 = NOME_QUADRO;
RENAME NOME_NIVEL1 = NOME_NIVEL;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PF'
			    WHEN T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T4.DOCCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING AND
                     t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (.,0,2) THEN 'SOCIO PJ' ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (.) THEN 0 ELSE t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO END) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO1

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT t1.NUCONSULTADO, t1.NOME_NIVEL,t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
                 FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES T1) T3
			ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T3.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
                 AND T1.NOME_QUADRO = T3.NOME_NIVEL)
	  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT T1.NUCONSULTADO,t1.DOCCONSULTADO FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
                 WHERE t1.DOCCONSULTADO NOT IN (.,0,3,11)) T4
			ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T4.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T4.DOCCONSULTADO)

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DOC_NUM_QUADRO1 = DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_EXCLUIR_NOMES T3 ON (T1.NOME_QUADRO = T3.NAME)
	  where T3.NAME IS MISSING

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES (WHERE=(DOCCONSULTADO>1));
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP TIPO_PESSOA__C ID_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   		SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*

		FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1 INNER JOIN
             (SELECT DISTINCT t1.docConsultado, (MAX(t1.DTH_CONSULTA)) FORMAT=DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA
                     FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1  GROUP BY t1.docConsultado) T2
        ON (T1.DTH_CONSULTA = T2.DTH_CONSULTA AND T1.docConsultado = T2.docConsultado)
        
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		 (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

	FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM AND T1.DOCCONSULTADO > 0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) AND t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL > 0)
	LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO > 0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) AND t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t3.DOCCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN T3.TP_QUADRO
		        ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN t3.DOCCONSULTADO IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN T3.DESCRICAO_QSA
		        ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA1

	FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT t1.DOCCONSULTADO,t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,t1.NOME_QUADRO,t1.TP_QUADRO,T1.DESCRICAO_QSA FROM WORK.PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES t1
               WHERE t1.TP_QUADRO IN ('SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PJ', 'SOCIO PJ')) t3
               ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t3.DOCCONSULTADO AND T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO
                   AND T1.NOME_QUADRO = T3.NOME_QUADRO)

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t6.CNAE,
      t6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
       (CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
             ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
	  (CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA = 2046 THEN 1
            WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
            WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
            ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN(2054,2046) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     
     (CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T6.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &DISPENSADOS. THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Dispensados
	 

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES T1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	LEFT JOIN PESQUISA_CNAE_GCP_ND t6 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t6.NUMCPFCNPJ)
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
	  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
            ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES T1

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.ID_PRINCIPAL,
          T1.NUCONSULTADO,
		  T1.NOME,
		  T1.NOM_BUREAUX,
          t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
          (SUM(t1.PartQuadro)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao,
		  count(tp_quadro) as qt_quadro

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
      GROUP BY t1.ID_PRINCIPAL,
               T1.NUCONSULTADO,
               t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
           t1.ID_PRINCIPAL,
           T1.NUCONSULTADO,
           T1.NOME,
		   T1.NOM_BUREAUX,
          
            FLOOR((MEAN(t1.Participacao))) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao
      FROM WORK.PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYP t1
      GROUP BY t1.ID_PRINCIPAL, T1.NUCONSULTADO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          (case when T2.Participacao is missing then 0 else T2.Participacao end) as Participacao
      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES T1
      LEFT JOIN PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_KYP t2 ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO AND T1.ID_PRINCIPAL = T2.ID_PRINCIPAL)
      ;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          (CASE WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' AND t1.Participacao > 100 and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO > 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' AND t1.Participacao > 100 and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN (0,.) THEN 'SOCIO ESTRANGEIRO PJ'
				ELSE t1.TP_QUADRO END) format $100. as TP_QUADRO1

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1

;QUIT;

DATA PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
SET PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('S/INFO','PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Abertura,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_DiretoriaSa

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES T1
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_CLASS AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
            T1.Nuconsultado,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'GRUPO_BV'
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'ABERTO - DISPENSADO'
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_SA'
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 'FALTA DIRETORIA SA'
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_1QSA'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 'ADM PUBLICA'
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 ELSE 'FALTA_ABERTURA' END) FORMAT $50. AS FL_QSA,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 0
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 1
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 2
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 3
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 4
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 10
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 12
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 13
				 ELSE 14 END)AS FL

	FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1
	GROUP BY T1.Nuconsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_CLASS; BY Nuconsultado FL;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_CLASS NODUPKEY; BY Nuconsultado;RUN;
PROC FREQ DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t2.FL_QSA

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES T1
	LEFT JOIN PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES_CLASS t2 ON (t1.Nuconsultado = t2.Nuconsultado)

       	;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO_SA','ABERTO - DISPENSADO','GRUPO_BV','ABERTO_1QSA') then 0
	        WHEN t1.Participacao >= 100 then 0
			WHEN t1.Participacao >= 75 then 1
			ELSE 3 END) AS fl_risco_abertura

    FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t1

       	;
QUIT;



 

PROC SORT DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT AS
	SELECT
    *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES
GROUP BY Nuconsultado,FL_QSA,DOC_NUM_QUADRO,DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
HAVING Count(*) > 1 and DOC_NUM_QUADRO NOT IN (99999999000191,0) and DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL > 0
;
QUIT;

PROC FREQ DATA=PRINCIPAL_KYC_SALES_CLASS ; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES  t1
      LEFT JOIN BS_QSA_FIM t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN T2.fl_risco_abertura = T1.fl_risco_abertura THEN 0
		       WHEN T2.fl_risco_abertura > T1.fl_risco_abertura THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) = 1
		;

QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE BS_QSA_FIM  AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM BS_QSA_FIM  t1
      LEFT JOIN PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
      WHERE t2.DocConsultado IS MISSING;
QUIT;

DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
FORMAT TP_QUADRO $24.;
FORMAT NOME_QUADRO $200.;
FORMAT NOME $200.;
FORMAT FL_QSA $50.;
FORMAT NOM_BUREAUX $24.;
FORMAT DESCRICAO_QSA $100.;
SET BS_QSA_FIM PRINCIPAL_KYP_SALES;
DROP NOME_NIVEL ID_PRINCIPAL PartQuadro;
RUN;

DATA BS_QSA_FIM;
SET BS_QSA_FIM;
IF DOCCONSULTADO = 10483444004842 AND DOC_NUM_QUADRO = 44095023368 AND TP_QUADRO = 'DIRETORIA' THEN DELETE;RUN;

RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=BS_QSA_FIM NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_NOME AS
	SELECT
    *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_QSA_FIM
GROUP BY NuConsultado,DOC_NUM_QUADRO,NOME,NOME_QUADRO,DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL
HAVING Count(*) > 1 and DOC_NUM_QUADRO not in (99999999999999,0,.) and DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL not in (99999999999999,0,.)
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_FLAG AS
	SELECT DISTINCT
    FL_QSA,NOM_BUREAUX,NuConsultado

FROM BS_QSA_FIM
;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_FLAG AS
	SELECT DISTINCT
    *,Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM BS_COUNT_FLAG
GROUP BY NuConsultado
HAVING Count(*) > 1
;
QUIT;

LIBNAME PLD '/riscosbvf/pld';
PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QSA_INI AS
    SELECT DISTINCT
      T1.NUCPFCNPJ,
	  t1.NOME,
	  T1.ESTEIRA,
	  (CASE WHEN T2.FL_QSA IS NOT MISSING THEN T2.FL_QSA ELSE T3.FL_QSA END) AS FL_QSA,
	  (CASE WHEN T2.DTH_CONSULTA IS NOT MISSING THEN T2.DTH_CONSULTA ELSE T3.DTH_CONSULTA END) format date9. AS DTH_CONSULTA,
	  T1.TpPessoa,
	  T1.ELEGIVEL

      FROM PLD.BS_360_FINAL T1
      LEFT JOIN PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE T2 ON (T2.NUCONSULTADO = T1.NUCPFCNPJ)
	  LEFT JOIN PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE T3 ON (INPUT(SUBSTR(T3.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.) = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCPFCNPJ,1,8),BEST14.));
	  WHERE t1.TpPessoa = 'J';
;
QUIT;

DATA PESQUISA_QSA_INI (WHERE=(TpPessoa = 'J'));
SET PESQUISA_QSA_INI;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PESQUISA_QSA_INI; BY NUCPFCNPJ NOME DESCENDING DTH_CONSULTA;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=PESQUISA_QSA_INI NODUPKEY; BY NUCPFCNPJ NOME;RUN;


PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_BV AS
     SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NUCPFCNPJ AS NUCONSULTADO

      FROM PESQUISA_QSA_INI t1
      WHERE t1.ELEGIVEL IN ('FUNDO EXCLUSIVO','NOVO','SIM') AND t1.FL_QSA IS MISSING
	  AND T1.NUCPFCNPJ NOT IN ('00000000000002','00000000000012','00000000000014','00000000000016')

UNION ALL

	  SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NUCPFCNPJ AS NUCONSULTADO

      FROM PESQUISA_QSA_INI t1
      WHERE T1.DTH_CONSULTA <&REF_INI. AND T1.NUCPFCNPJ NOT IN ('00000000000002','00000000000012','00000000000014','00000000000016')
	  AND T1.FL_QSA IN ('FUNDO EXCLUSIVO','NOVO','SIM')

;quit;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_BANKLY AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
     PUT(T2.cd_cpf_cnpj,Z14.) AS NUCONSULTADO

      FROM Base_Ativa_Bankly_PJ t2
	  LEFT JOIN PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE t3 ON (T2.cd_cpf_cnpj = T3.DOCCONSULTADO)
      WHERE t3.FL_QSA IS MISSING

UNION ALL

	 SELECT DISTINCT
     PUT(T2.cd_cpf_cnpj,Z14.) AS NUCONSULTADO

      FROM Base_Ativa_Bankly_PJ t2
	  LEFT JOIN PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE t3 ON (T2.cd_cpf_cnpj = T3.DOCCONSULTADO)
      WHERE T3.DTH_CONSULTA <&REF_INI.;

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_DOC_NUM AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
      PUT(t2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.) AS NUCONSULTADO

      FROM PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE t2
	  INNER JOIN Base_Ativa_Bankly_PJ T1 ON (T1.cd_cpf_cnpj = T2.DOCCONSULTADO)
      WHERE t2.FL_QSA IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA','FALTA_ABERTURA') AND t2.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA'

UNION ALL

	 SELECT DISTINCT
      PUT(t2.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.) AS NUCONSULTADO

      FROM PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE t2
	  INNER JOIN PESQUISA_QSA_INI T1 ON (T2.NUCONSULTADO = T1.NUCPFCNPJ)
      WHERE t2.FL_QSA IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA','FALTA_ABERTURA') AND t2.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA'

;QUIT;


DATA PESQUISA_QUADRO;
SET PESQUISA_DOC_NUM PESQUISA_QUADRO_BANKLY PESQUISA_QUADRO_BV;RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PESQUISA_QUADRO NODUPKEY;BY _ALL_;RUN;


  proc sql noprint;
    select distinct
           "'" || NUCONSULTADO || "'"
      into :cnpj_list separated by ','
    from WORK.PESQUISA_QUADRO
    where not missing(NUCONSULTADO);
  quit;

  %put &cnpj_list;

PROC SQL;
   connect to bigquery as db2 (project='bv-sald-dtm' credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/ld/sald-dtm.json'
      schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage);

   create table BASE_CNPJ_NOVO as
   select * from connection to db2 (
      SELECT
         t1.CNPJ AS NumCpfCnpj,
         LEFT(REGEXP_REPLACE(t1.CNPJ, '[^0-9]', ''), 8) AS NUM_RAIZ,
         t1.RAZAO_SOCIAL AS NOME,
         t1.CNAE,
         t1.NATUREZA_JURIDICA_ENR AS COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
         t1.SITUACAO_CADASTRAL AS STATUS,
         T1.tsinclusao
      FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_cdsp.tbcadastralserasapj`  t1
	  WHERE t1.CNPJ IN (&cnpj_list)
   );

   disconnect from db2;
quit;

data BASE_CNPJ_NOVO;
set BASE_CNPJ_NOVO;
   data_hora = input(tsinclusao, anydtdtm.);
   format data_hora datetime20.;
run;

data BASE_CNPJ_NOVO;
set BASE_CNPJ_NOVO;
drop tsinclusao;
rename data_hora = tsinclusao;
run;

PROC SORT DATA=BASE_CNPJ_NOVO; BY NumCpfCnpj DESCENDING tsinclusao;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=BASE_CNPJ_NOVO NODUPKEY; BY NumCpfCnpj;RUN;



PROC SQL;
   connect to bigquery as db2 (project='bv-sald-dtm' credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/ld/sald-dtm.json'
      schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage);

   create table PesquisaQSA as
   select * from connection to db2 (
      SELECT
         t1.cnpj_consultado,
         (CASE WHEN t1.tipo_pessoa = 'F' THEN 'SOCIO PF'
               WHEN t1.tipo_pessoa = 'J' THEN 'PRINCIPAL QSA' ELSE 'S/INFO' END) AS TP_QUADRO,
         t1.documento_socio AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
         t1.nome AS NOME_QUADRO,
         t1.capital_total,
		 t1.cnpj_consultado AS DOC_NUM_NIVEL,
         t1.tsinclusao
      FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_corp_credito.tbquadrosocietarioserasa`  t1
	  WHERE t1.cnpj_consultado IN (&cnpj_list)
   );
   disconnect from db2;
quit;

   PROC SQL;
      CREATE TABLE PesquisaQSA AS
      SELECT DISTINCT
      	*,
        (CASE WHEN DOC_NUM_QUADRO = '014591453' THEN '1459145000118'
              WHEN DOC_NUM_QUADRO = '081138562' THEN '8113856000185'
              ELSE  DOC_NUM_QUADRO END) AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO1

      FROM PesquisaQSA
;  QUIT;

DATA PesquisaQSA;
SET PesquisaQSA;
DROP DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RENAME DOC_NUM_QUADRO1 = DOC_NUM_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PesquisaQSA NODUPKEY;BY _ALL_;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURAQSA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
            T1.cnpj_consultado,
			(SUM(t1.capital_total)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao,
			T1.tsinclusao

      FROM PesquisaQSA t1
	  WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('DIRETORIA','PRINCIPAL QSA')
      GROUP BY T1.cnpj_consultado,T1.tsinclusao;
QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=PERCENTUAL_ABERTURAQSA; BY cnpj_consultado DESCENDING tsinclusao;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=PERCENTUAL_ABERTURAQSA NODUPKEY; BY cnpj_consultado;RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PesquisaQSA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
            T1.*

      FROM PesquisaQSA t1
	  INNER JOIN PERCENTUAL_ABERTURAQSA T2 ON (T1.cnpj_consultado = T2.cnpj_consultado
                                               AND T1.tsinclusao = T2.tsinclusao)

;QUIT;



   
   PROC SQL;
      CREATE TABLE PrincipalQSA AS
      SELECT DISTINCT DOC_NUM_QUADRO,NOME_QUADRO
      FROM PesquisaQSA
      WHERE TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA';
   QUIT;

   
   PROC SQL noprint;
      SELECT DISTINCT "'" || strip(DOC_NUM_QUADRO) || "'"
      INTO :doc_list separated by ","
      FROM PrincipalQSA;
   QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   connect to bigquery as db2 (
      project='bv-sald-dtm'
      credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/ld/sald-dtm.json'
      schema='bv-pdbd-prd'
      READ_MODE=storage
   );

   create table ReconsultaPrincipalQSA as
   select * from connection to db2 (
      SELECT
         cnpj_consultado,
         (CASE WHEN tipo_pessoa = 'F' THEN 'SOCIO PF'
               WHEN tipo_pessoa = 'J' THEN 'PRINCIPAL QSA' ELSE 'S/INFO' END) AS TP_QUADRO,
         documento_socio AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
         nome AS NOME_QUADRO,
         capital_total,
         cnpj_consultado AS DOC_NUM_NIVEL,
         tsinclusao
      FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_corp_credito.tbquadrosocietarioserasa`
      WHERE cnpj_consultado IN (&doc_list)
   );

   disconnect from db2;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
            T1.cnpj_consultado,
			(SUM(t1.capital_total)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao,
			T1.tsinclusao

      FROM ReconsultaPrincipalQSA t1
	  WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('DIRETORIA','PRINCIPAL QSA')
      GROUP BY T1.cnpj_consultado,T1.tsinclusao;
QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA; BY cnpj_consultado DESCENDING tsinclusao;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA NODUPKEY; BY cnpj_consultado;RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
          INPUT(T1.NuConsultado,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DocConsultado,
		  T1.NuConsultado,
		  T4.NOME FORMAT $150. AS NOME,
		  INPUT(t4.CNAE,BEST8.) AS CNAE,
		  INPUT(t4.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,BEST4.) AS COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
          t4.STATUS as ST_RECEITA,
		  T3.NOME_QUADRO,
		  INPUT(t3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
          t3.TP_QUADRO FORMAT $24. AS TP_QUADRO,
		  'SERASA' FORMAT $24. AS NOM_BUREAUX,
		  datepart(T3.tsinclusao) FORMAT DATE9. AS DTH_CONSULTA,
		  T3.capital_total  as PartQuadro

	FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO t1
	INNER JOIN PesquisaQSA T3 ON (T3.cnpj_consultado = T1.NuConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BASE_CNPJ_NOVO T4 ON (T4.NumCpfcnpj = T1.NuConsultado)
    WHERE t3.TP_QUADRO NOT = 'S/INFO'

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          (CASE WHEN t1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO_PJ' ELSE t1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS TP_QUADRO1

      FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t1
      LEFT JOIN PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA t2 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = INPUT(t2.cnpj_consultado,BEST14.) AND T2.Participacao > 99);
QUIT;

DATA PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
SET PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE ReconsultaPrincipalQSAFim AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t3.DocConsultado,
          t3.NuConsultado,
          t3.NOME,
          t3.CNAE,
          t3.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA,
          t3.ST_RECEITA,
          t1.TP_QUADRO,
          INPUT(t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,BEST14.) FORMAT BEST14. AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO,
          t1.NOME_QUADRO,
          (T1.capital_total*t3.PartQuadro)/100 AS PartQuadro,
          t3.NOM_BUREAUX,
          t3.DTH_CONSULTA

 FROM ReconsultaPrincipalQSA T1
 LEFT JOIN PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t3 ON (INPUT(t1.cnpj_consultado,BEST14.) = T3.DOC_NUM_QUADRO)

;
QUIT;

DATA PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
SET PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP ReconsultaPrincipalQSAFim;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		  t1.docConsultado AS DOC_NUM_QUADRO_NIVEL,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos,
		  (CASE WHEN t4.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_Principal_Fundos_Exclusivo,
		  (CASE WHEN t5.docConsultado IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_QSA_Fundo,
          (CASE WHEN t5.FUNDO_EXCLUSIVO_CALC = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)  AS Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo

	FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t1
 	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t4 ON (t1.docConsultado = t4.docConsultado)
	LEFT JOIN BS_FUNDOS_CADASTRO_FIM t5 ON (t1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t5.docConsultado)

;QUIT;


PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS DESCRICAO_QSA,
		  (CASE WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
		        WHEN T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T7.Name IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL IS NOT MISSING AND T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA', 'SOCIO PJ') THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $100. AS TP_QUADRO1

	FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t1
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t2.DOC_NUM and T1.DOCCONSULTADO>0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T3 ON (t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(T1.NUCONSULTADO,1,8),BEST14.) and t3.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL>0)
	LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t4 ON (T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO = t4.DOC_NUM and T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO >0)
    LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T5 ON (t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(PUT(T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO,Z14.),1,8),BEST14.) and t5.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL>0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t6 ON (T1.DOCCONSULTADO = t6.'CPF/CNPJ'n AND T6.'CPF/CNPJ'n > 0)
	LEFT JOIN listado_bolsa_sales t7 ON (T1.nome_quadro = t7.Name AND T1.doc_num_quadro IN (0,.))

;QUIT;

DATA PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
SET PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
DROP TP_QUADRO;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RUN;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   	SELECT DISTINCT
			T1.*,
			(CASE WHEN T1.DESCRICAO_QSA IN ('CNPJ RAIZ DO PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA','PJ LISTADO EM BOLSA') THEN 1
             ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta_Qsa,
          	(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA = 2046 THEN 1
                                WHEN t2.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                         		WHEN t4.DOC_NUM IS NOT MISSING THEN 1
                	     	    ELSE 0 END)> 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SAberta,
	 		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN(2054,2046) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_SA_FIM,
     		(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.docConsultado IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
               			 		WHEN T1.DOC_NUM_QUADRO IN &GRUPO_BV. THEN 1
            		     		ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS GRUPO_BV_FIM,
     	 	(CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &DISPENSADOS. THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) >0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Dispensados

      FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t1
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA t2 ON (t1.DocConsultado = t2.DOC_NUM)
	  LEFT JOIN BS_LISTADA_BOLSA T4 ON (t4.NUM_RAIZ_PRINCIPAL = INPUT(SUBSTR(t1.NuConsultado,1,8),BEST14.))
	  GROUP BY T1.NuConsultado

;QUIT;



 PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
		 (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
			   WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
               ELSE T1.TP_QUADRO END) FORMAT $150. AS  TP_QUADRO1,
		  (CASE WHEN T1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.TP_QUADRO = 'PRINCIPAL QSA' THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
				WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('PRINCIPAL QSA','FALTA DIRETORIA SA') AND T1.Fl_QSA_Fundo_Exclusivo = 0 THEN 'SOCIO PJ'
                ELSE T1.DESCRICAO_QSA END) FORMAT $150. AS  DESCRICAO_QSA1

     FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t1

 ;QUIT;

DATA PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
SET PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
DROP TP_QUADRO DESCRICAO_QSA ;
RENAME TP_QUADRO1 = TP_QUADRO;
RENAME DESCRICAO_QSA1 = DESCRICAO_QSA;
RUN;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_GCP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.NUCONSULTADO,
		  T1.NOME,
		  T1.NOM_BUREAUX,
		  (SUM(t1.PartQuadro)) FORMAT=BEST5. AS Participacao

      FROM WORK.PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t1
	  WHERE T1.TP_QUADRO NOT IN ('DIRETORIA','PRINCIPAL QSA')
      GROUP BY t1.NUCONSULTADO;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          t1.*,
          (case when T2.Participacao is missing then 0 else T2.Participacao end) as Participacao

      FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP T1
      LEFT JOIN PERCENTUAL_ABERTURA_GCP t2 ON (T1.NUCONSULTADO = T2.NUCONSULTADO)
      ;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('S/INFO','PRINCIPAL QSA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_Abertura,
	  (CASE WHEN (SUM(CASE WHEN T1.TP_QUADRO IN ('FALTA DIRETORIA SA') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END))>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Fl_DiretoriaSa

    FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP T1
    GROUP BY t1.DocConsultado

;QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE QSA_GCP_CLASS AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.Nuconsultado,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 'GRUPO_BV'
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 'ABERTO - DISPENSADO'
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_SA'
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 'FALTA DIRETORIA SA'
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO_1QSA'
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 'ADM PUBLICA'
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 'ABERTO'
				 ELSE 'FALTA_ABERTURA' END) FORMAT $50. AS FL_QSA,
		   (CASE WHEN T1.GRUPO_BV_FIM = 1 THEN 0
                 WHEN T1.Fl_Dispensados = 1 THEN 1
	             WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 2
              	 WHEN t1.Fl_SAberta_Qsa = 1 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 AND T1.Fl_DiretoriaSa = 1 THEN 3
				 WHEN T1.Participacao >= 75 AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 4
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN (4014,2305,2313,2135) AND T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 10
				 WHEN T1.COD_NATUREZA_JURIDICA IN &ADM_PUBLICA. THEN 12
				 WHEN T1.Fl_Abertura = 0 THEN 13
				 ELSE 14 END)AS FL

	FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t1
	GROUP BY T1.Nuconsultado

;QUIT;

PROC SORT DATA=QSA_GCP_CLASS; BY Nuconsultado FL;RUN;
PROC SORT DATA=QSA_GCP_CLASS NODUPKEY; BY Nuconsultado;RUN;
PROC FREQ DATA=QSA_GCP_CLASS; TABLES FL_QSA; RUN ;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE  PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  t2.FL_QSA

    FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP T1
	LEFT JOIN QSA_GCP_CLASS t2 ON (t1.Nuconsultado = t2.Nuconsultado)

       	;
QUIT;

DATA PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
SET PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
DROP ST_RECEITAF PartQuadro;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_; RUN;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
    SELECT
      T1.*,
	  (CASE WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO_SA','ABERTO - DISPENSADO','GRUPO_BV','ABERTO_1QSA') then 0
	        WHEN t1.Participacao >= 100 then 0
	        WHEN t1.FL_QSA	IN ('ABERTO') and t1.Participacao = 0 then 2
			WHEN t1.Participacao >= 75 then 1
		    ELSE 3 END) AS fl_risco_abertura

    FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP T1
       	;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
	CREATE TABLE BS_COUNT_NOME AS
	SELECT
    *,
    Count(*) AS QTD_DUPLICADO

FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP
GROUP BY NuConsultado,DOC_NUM_QUADRO,NOME,NOME_QUADRO
HAVING Count(*) > 1
;
QUIT;



PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP  t1
      LEFT JOIN PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE (CASE WHEN t2.DocConsultado is NOT missing AND T1.DTH_CONSULTA <=  T2.DTH_CONSULTA THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 0;

QUIT;

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE AS
   SELECT DISTINCT
          T1.*

      FROM PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE t1
      LEFT JOIN PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP t2 ON (t1.docConsultado = t2.DocConsultado)
	  WHERE t2.DocConsultado IS MISSING
	  ;

QUIT;

DATA PLD.BS_QSA_FIM;
FORMAT TP_QUADRO $24.;
FORMAT NOME_QUADRO $200.;
FORMAT NOME $200.;
FORMAT FL_QSA $50.;
FORMAT NOM_BUREAUX $24.;
FORMAT DESCRICAO_QSA $100.;
SET PLD.BS_QSA_FIM_TESTE PESQUISA_QUADRO_GCP;
DROP PcParticipacao ST_RECEITA;
RUN;

PROC SORT DATA=PLD.BS_QSA_FIM NODUPKEY; BY _ALL_; RUN;

DATA PLD.BS_QSA_FIM;
SET PLD.BS_QSA_FIM;
if docconsultado = 1731963815 then delete;
RUN;

DATA PLD.BS_QSA_FIM;
SET PLD.BS_QSA_FIM;
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = upcase(NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÍÌÎÏ]/I/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÓÒÔÕÖ]/O/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME_QUADRO);
    NOME_QUADRO = strip(NOME_QUADRO);
;RUN;

DATA PLD.BS_QSA_FIM;
SET PLD.BS_QSA_FIM;
    NOME = prxchange('s/[.,!-,‘’.]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/-/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\d+//', -1,NOME);
    NOME = upcase(NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[áàâãä]/a/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[éèêë]/e/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[íìîï]/i/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[óòôõö]/o/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[úùûü]/u/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÁÀÂÃÄ]/A/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÉÈÊË]/E/', -1,NOME);

    NOME = prxchange('s/[ÚÙÛÜ]/U/', -1,NOME);
    NOME = prxchange('s/\s+/ /', -1,NOME);
    NOME = strip(NOME);
;RUN;
